diff --git a/src/app/media-checker/case-list/CasesListClient.js b/src/app/media-checker/case-list/CasesListClient.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a2e106e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/app/media-checker/case-list/CasesListClient.js
@@ -0,0 +1,215 @@
+'use client'
+
+import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
+import { Clock, BadgeCheck, ExternalLink, Clapperboard, AudioLines, Image as ImageIcon } from "lucide-react";
+// import { CaseRow, GRID_CLASS } from "./CaseRow"; // Import the row and grid class
+import { fetchMoreCases } from "./actions"; // Import the server action
+import Link from "next/link";
+import { PlusCircle, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
+
+const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 20;
+
+
+// Shared Grid Layout
+export const GRID_CLASS = "grid grid-cols-[40px_minmax(200px,3fr)_120px_120px_120px_2fr_100px_80px] gap-4 items-center px-6";
+
+const CaseRow = ({ val, idx, user }) => {
+    const time = new Date(val.created_at);
+    const { analysis_types, upload_type } = val.input_request;
+    const { name } = val.file_metadata;
+
+    // Logic from original code
+    const has_result = typeof (val.prediction) === "boolean";
+    const is_done = val.status === true || has_result;
+
+    // Logic for text display
+    const prediction_text = (is_done || user.verifier) && (val.prediction !== null)
+        ? (val.prediction ? "Real" : "Fake")
+        : "---";
+
+    const method = val.method;
+    const status = val.status;
+
+    return (
+        <div className={`${GRID_CLASS} py-4 border-b border-zinc-50 hover:bg-zinc-50/50 transition-colors group text-sm text-zinc-600 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-2 duration-500`}>
+
+            {/* SNO */}
+            <span className='font-mono text-zinc-400 text-center'>{idx + 1}</span>
+
+            {/* NAME */}
+            <span className='truncate font-medium text-zinc-700 pr-4' title={name}>
+                {name || "Untitled File"}
+            </span>
+
+            {/* UPLOAD TYPE */}
+            <span className='flex items-center gap-2 capitalize text-zinc-500'>
+                {upload_type === "video" && <Clapperboard className='size-4' />}
+                {upload_type === "audio" && <AudioLines className='size-4' />}
+                {upload_type === "image" && <ImageIcon className='size-4' />}
+                {upload_type}
+            </span>
+
+            {/* STATUS */}
+            <div>
+                {method === "direct" ? (
+                    <div className={`flex items-center w-fit gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border ${(is_done && has_result)
+                        ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200"
+                        : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200"
+                        }`}>
+                        {(is_done && has_result)
+                            ? <BadgeCheck className='size-3.5' />
+                            : <Clock className='size-3.5' />
+                        }
+                        {(is_done && has_result) ? "Complete" : "Pending"}
+                        {typeof (val.prediction) === "boolean"}
+                    </div>
+                ) : (
+                    <div className={`flex items-center w-fit gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border ${(status)
+                        ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200"
+                        : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200"
+                        }`}>
+                        {(status)
+                            ? <BadgeCheck className='size-3.5' />
+                            : <Clock className='size-3.5' />
+                        }
+                        {status ? "Complete" : "Pending"}
+                    </div>
+                )}
+            </div>
+
+            {/* PREDICTION */}
+            <div>
+                <div className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium w-fit border ${prediction_text === "Real"
+                    ? "bg-green-50 text-green-700 border-green-200"
+                    : prediction_text === "Fake"
+                        ? "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200"
+                        : "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-400 border-zinc-200"
+                    }`}>
+                    {prediction_text}
+                </div>
+            </div>
+
+            {/* ANALYSIS TAGS */}
+            <div className='flex flex-wrap gap-2'>
+                {Object.keys(analysis_types).map((input_type, input_idx) => {
+                    if (analysis_types[input_type])
+                        return (
+                            <span key={input_idx} className='text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500 bg-white border border-zinc-200 rounded-md px-2 py-0.5'>
+                                {input_type}
+                            </span>
+                        )
+                })}
+            </div>
+
+            {/* DATE */}
+            <span className='text-zinc-400 text-xs'>
+                {time.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })}
+            </span>
+
+            {/* LINK */}
+            <span className="flex justify-end pr-2">
+                {val.prediction !== null ? (
+                    <Link
+                        href={'/view/' + val.id}
+                        target="_blank"
+                        className='p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 transition-all'
+                    >
+                        <ExternalLink className='size-4' />
+                    </Link>
+                ) : (
+                    <span className="text-zinc-300 text-center block w-8">---</span>
+                )}
+            </span>
+        </div>
+    )
+}
+
+export default function CasesListClient({ initialData, user }) {
+    const [cases, setCases] = useState(initialData);
+    const [offset, setOffset] = useState(ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
+    const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(true);
+    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
+
+    // Ref for the bottom of the list
+    const observerTarget = useRef(null);
+
+    const loadMore = useCallback(async () => {
+        if (isLoading || !hasMore) return;
+
+        setIsLoading(true);
+        try {
+            // Call the server action
+            const newCases = await fetchMoreCases(offset, ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
+
+            if (newCases.length < ITEMS_PER_PAGE) {
+                setHasMore(false);
+            }
+
+            if (newCases.length > 0) {
+                setCases(prev => [...prev, ...newCases]);
+                setOffset(prev => prev + ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
+            }
+        } catch (error) {
+            console.error("Failed to load more cases", error);
+        } finally {
+            setIsLoading(false);
+        }
+    }, [offset, hasMore, isLoading]);
+
+    // Intersection Observer to detect scroll
+    useEffect(() => {
+        const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
+            entries => {
+                if (entries[0].isIntersecting) {
+                    loadMore();
+                }
+            },
+            { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: "100px" } // Load slightly before reaching bottom
+        );
+
+        if (observerTarget.current) {
+            observer.observe(observerTarget.current);
+        }
+
+        return () => {
+            if (observerTarget.current) {
+                observer.unobserve(observerTarget.current);
+            }
+        };
+    }, [loadMore]);
+
+    if (!cases || cases.length === 0) {
+        return (
+            <div className='flex flex-col w-full justify-center items-center py-24 text-zinc-400'>
+                <div className='bg-zinc-50 p-4 rounded-full mb-4'>
+                    <PlusCircle className="size-8 text-zinc-300" strokeWidth={1} />
+                </div>
+                <div className='text-lg font-light'>No cases found</div>
+                <Link href={'/media-checker'} className='mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-900 underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-zinc-600 transition-colors'>
+                    Start your first analysis
+                </Link>
+            </div>
+        );
+    }
+
+    return (

+            ))}
+
+            {/* Loading Indicator / Observer Target */}
+            <div ref={observerTarget} className="w-full py-8 flex justify-center items-center">
+                {isLoading && (
+                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-400 text-sm animate-pulse">
+                        <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
+                        Loading more cases...
+                    </div>
+                )}
+                {!hasMore && cases.length > 0 && (
+                    <div className="text-zinc-300 text-xs tracking-widest uppercase">End of list</div>
+                )}
+            </div>
+        </div>
+    )
+}

+
+import { FileUp, Check, X } from "lucide-react";
+import { PhotoVideo, Waveform, ImageSearch, LoadingCircle, RemoveBin, InfoCircle } from '@/components/SVGs';
+
+const Form = () => {
+
+    const [response_data, set_res_data] = useState({ got_result: false });
+    const [id, set_id] = useState("");
+
+    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
+    const [tempfileUrl, set_tempFileUrl] = useState(null);
+    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
+    const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false); // Added for UI state only
+
+    // FORM DATA
+    const [file, setfile] = useState(null);
+    const [analysisTypes, setAnalysisTypes] = useState({
+        frameCheck: false,
+        audioAnalysis: false,
+        aigcCheck: false
+    });
+    const [uploadType, setUploadType] = useState("");
+
+    //convert data to human filesize for metadata
+    const humanFileSize = (bytes, si = true, dp = 2) => {
+        const thresh = si ? 1000 : 1024;
+        if (Math.abs(bytes) < thresh) return bytes + ' B';
+        const units = si
+            ? ['kB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB', 'PB', 'EB', 'ZB', 'YB']
+            : ['KiB', 'MiB', 'GiB', 'TiB', 'PiB', 'EiB', 'ZiB', 'YiB'];
+        let u = -1;
+        const r = 10 ** dp;
+        do {
+            bytes /= thresh;
+            ++u;
+        } while (Math.round(Math.abs(bytes) * r) / r >= thresh && u < units.length - 1);
+        return bytes.toFixed(dp) + ' ' + units[u];
+    }
+
+    //''' confirm selected analysis are correct to use '''
+    const validate_analysis = (media_type) => {
+        console.log(media_type);
+        let new_analysis = { ...analysisTypes };
+
+        if (media_type.split("/")[0] === "video") {
+            setUploadType("video");
+            new_analysis["aigcCheck"] = false;
+        }
+        else if (media_type.split("/")[0] === "audio") {
+            setUploadType("audio");
+            new_analysis["frameCheck"] = false;
+            new_analysis["aigcCheck"] = false;
+            setAnalysisTypes(new_analysis);
+        }
+        else if (media_type.split("/")[0] === "image") {
+            setUploadType("image");
+            new_analysis["frameCheck"] = false;
+            new_analysis["audioAnalysis"] = false;
+        }
+        setAnalysisTypes(new_analysis);
+    }
+
+    const handleAnalysisTypeChange = (val) => {
+        let new_analysis = { ...analysisTypes }
+        new_analysis[val] = !new_analysis[val]
+        setAnalysisTypes(new_analysis);
+    };
+
+    // -------------------->>SUBMIT RESULT FUNCTIONS
+
+    const upload_file_s3 = async () => {
+        try {
+            const res = await fetch('/api/upload', {
+                method: 'POST',
+                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+                body: JSON.stringify(
+                    {
+                        input_request: {
+                            'upload_type': uploadType,
+                            'analysis_types': analysisTypes
+                        },
+                        file_metadata: {
+                            name: file.name,
+                            size: humanFileSize(file.size),
+                            type: file.type
+                        },
+                        method: "direct"
+                    }
+                ),
+            });
+            if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to get signed URL');
+            const { id, signedUrl } = await res.json();
+            const res_s3 = await fetch(signedUrl, {
+                method: 'PUT',
+                body: file,
+                headers: { 'Content-Type': file.type },
+            });
+            if (!res_s3.ok) throw new Error('Failed to upload file to S3');
+            const res_set_to_queue = await fetch('/api/send-message', {
+                method: 'POST',
+                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+                body: JSON.stringify(
+                    {
+                        task_id: id,
+                        method: "direct"
+                    }
+                ),
+            });
+            if (!res_set_to_queue.ok) throw new Error('Failed to send message to queue');
+            alert('File Uploaded');
+            return id;
+        } catch (error) {
+            console.error('Error uploading file:', error);
+            alert('Failed to upload file');
+        }
+    }
+
+    const get_server_response = async () => {
+        try {
+            const supabase_id = await upload_file_s3();
+            let res_data = {
+                "uploaded": true,
+                "got_result": false,
+            };
+            set_res_data(res_data);
+            set_id(supabase_id);
+        }
+        catch (error) {
+            console.error("Error in sending data", error);
+        }
+        setLoading(false);
+    };
+
+    const handle_submit = (e) => {
+        e.preventDefault();
+        if (file === null)
+            alert("File not uploaded");
+        else if (analysisTypes['frameCheck'] === false && analysisTypes["audioAnalysis"] === false && analysisTypes['aigcCheck'] === false)
+            alert('please choose atleast one analysis')
+        else {
+            setLoading(true);
+            get_server_response();
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Form functions
+    const handleFileChange = (event) => {
+        const newfile = event.target.files[0];
+        if (newfile === undefined) {
+            setUploadType("")
+        }
+        const maxSize = 50 * 1024 * 1024;
+        if (newfile.size > maxSize) {
+            alert("File size exceeds 50MB. Please choose a smaller file.");
+            setUploadType("");
+            setfile(null);
+            set_tempFileUrl(null);
+            return;
+        }
+        validate_analysis(newfile.type);
+        setfile(newfile);
+        set_tempFileUrl(URL.createObjectURL(newfile));
+    };
+
+    const handleDrop = (event) => {
+        event.preventDefault();
+        setIsDragging(false);
+        const droppedFile = event.dataTransfer.files[0];
+        const maxSize = 50 * 1024 * 1024;
+        if (droppedFile && droppedFile.size > maxSize) {
+            alert("File size exceeds 50MB. Please choose a smaller file.");
+            setUploadType("");
+            setfile(null);
+            set_tempFileUrl(null);
+            return;
+        }
+        if (droppedFile && (droppedFile.type.startsWith('video/') || droppedFile.type.startsWith('audio/') || droppedFile.type.startsWith('image/'))) {
+            setfile(droppedFile);
+            set_tempFileUrl(URL.createObjectURL(droppedFile));
+            validate_analysis(droppedFile.type)
+            console.log(droppedFile);
+        } else {
+            alert('Please select a valid video/audio/image file.');
+        }
+    };
+
+    const handleDragOver = (event) => {
+        event.preventDefault();
+        setIsDragging(true);
+    };
+
+    const handleDragLeave = (event) => {
+        event.preventDefault();
+        setIsDragging(false);
+    }
+
+    const removeFile = (e) => {
+        e.preventDefault(); // prevent form submit if inside form
+        setfile(null);
+        set_tempFileUrl(null);
+        setUploadType("");
+        fileInputRef.current.value = '';
+    };
+
+    // Helper component for Analysis Cards
+    const AnalysisCard = ({ active, onClick, icon: Icon, title, desc }) => (
+        <div
+            onClick={onClick}
+            className={`
+                group relative flex flex-col justify-between p-5 h-48 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer transition-all duration-300 border
+                ${active
+                    ? 'border-primary bg-primary/5 shadow-inner'
+                    : 'border-zinc-200 bg-white hover:border-zinc-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:-translate-y-1'
+                }
+            `}
+        >
+            <div className="flex justify-between items-start w-full z-10">
+                <div className={`p-2 rounded-xl transition-colors ${active ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-400 group-hover:text-primary group-hover:bg-primary/10'}`}>
+                    <Icon className="size-6" strokeWidth={active ? 2 : 1.5} />
+                </div>
+                {active && (
+                    <div className="bg-primary text-white rounded-full p-1 animate-in zoom-in duration-300">
+                        <Check className="size-3" strokeWidth={3} />
+                    </div>
+                )}
+            </div>
+
+            <div className="z-10 mt-4">
+                <h3 className={`text-lg font-semibold ${active ? 'text-primary' : 'text-zinc-700'}`}>{title}</h3>
+                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 mt-1 leading-relaxed">{desc}</p>
+            </div>
+
+            {/* Background Decoration */}
+            <div className={`absolute -bottom-4 -right-4 opacity-[0.03] pointer-events-none transition-all duration-500 ${active ? 'scale-110 opacity-[0.07] text-primary' : 'text-zinc-500'}`}>
+                <Icon className="size-32" />
+            </div>
+        </div>
+    );
+
+    return (
+        <form onSubmit={handle_submit} className="max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-8 px-6 py-8 min-h-[600px] animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-500">
+
+            {/* --- LEFT COLUMN: Upload & Preview --- */}
+            <div className='flex-1 flex flex-col gap-6'>
+                <div className={`
+                    relative w-full flex-grow min-h-[50vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl overflow-hidden transition-all duration-500 border border-zinc-200
+                    ${!file ? 'bg-zinc-50/50' : 'bg-black'}
+                `}>
+                    {file ? (
+                        // FILE PREVIEW STATE
+                        <div className="relative w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-zinc-900">
+                            {file.type.startsWith('video/') ? (
+                                <video className='w-full max-h-[60vh] object-contain' controls src={tempfileUrl}></video>
+                            ) : file.type.startsWith('audio/') ? (
+                                <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-6 p-10 w-full max-w-md">
+                                    <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center animate-pulse">
+                                        <Waveform className="size-10 text-zinc-400" />
+                                    </div>
+                                    <audio className='w-full' controls src={tempfileUrl}></audio>
+                                </div>
+                            ) : file.type.startsWith('image/') ? (
+                                <div className="relative w-full h-full">
+                                    <Image
+                                        src={tempfileUrl}
+                                        fill
+                                        className="object-contain"
+                                        alt='uploaded_preview'
+                                    />
+                                </div>
+                            ) : (
+                                <div className='text-red-400 font-medium bg-red-950/30 px-6 py-3 rounded-full border border-red-900'>Invalid File Type</div>
+                            )}
+
+                            {/* Floating Metadata Card */}
+                            <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 right-4 flex justify-between items-start pointer-events-none">
+                                <div className="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-md text-white border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-4 flex gap-4 items-center max-w-[80%] pointer-events-auto">
+                                    <div className="bg-white/10 p-2 rounded-lg">
+                                        <FileUp className="size-5" />
+                                    </div>
+                                    <div className="overflow-hidden">
+                                        <p className="font-medium text-sm truncate max-w-[200px]">{file.name}</p>
+                                        <p className="text-xs text-white/60 font-mono mt-0.5">{humanFileSize(file.size)}</p>
+                                    </div>
+                                </div>
+                                <button
+                                    onClick={removeFile}
+                                    className="pointer-events-auto bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-md hover:bg-red-500/20 hover:text-red-400 text-white/60 border border-white/10 rounded-full p-3 transition-all duration-200"
+                                >
+                                    <X className="size-5" />
+                                </button>
+                            </div>
+                        </div>
+                    ) : (
+                        // EMPTY UPLOAD STATE
+                        <div
+                            className={`
+                                flex flex-col justify-center items-center w-full h-full cursor-pointer transition-all duration-300
+                                ${isDragging ? 'bg-primary/5 border-primary scale-[0.99]' : 'hover:bg-zinc-100/50'}
+                            `}
+                            onDrop={handleDrop}
+                            onDragOver={handleDragOver}
+                            onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
+                            onClick={() => { fileInputRef.current.click() }}
+                        >
+                            <div className="relative">
+                                <div className={`absolute inset-0 bg-primary blur-2xl opacity-20 rounded-full transition-opacity duration-500 ${isDragging ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`} />
+                                <div className="relative bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-zinc-100">
+                                    <FileUp className={`size-10 ${isDragging ? 'text-primary' : 'text-zinc-400'}`} strokeWidth={1.5} />
+                                </div>
+                            </div>
+
+                            <h3 className="mt-8 text-xl font-medium text-zinc-800">Upload Media</h3>
+                            <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-500 text-sm">Drag & drop or <span className="text-primary font-medium underline decoration-primary/30 underline-offset-4">browse</span></p>
+
+                            <div className="mt-8 flex gap-4 text-xs text-zinc-400 font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
+                                <span>Video</span>
+                                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-zinc-300 self-center" />
+                                <span>Audio</span>
+                                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-zinc-300 self-center" />
+                                <span>Image</span>
+                            </div>
+                        </div>
+                    )}
+
+                    <input
+                        ref={fileInputRef}
+                        type="file"
+                        accept={`video/*, audio/*, image/*`}
+                        onChange={handleFileChange}
+                        className='hidden'
+                    />
+                </div>
+
+                {/* Submit Action Area */}
+                <div className="flex justify-between items-center px-2">
+                    <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400 hidden sm:block">
+                        {loading && "Processing secure upload..."}
+                    </div>
+
+                    <button
+                        disabled={(loading || (response_data.uploaded && !response_data.got_result))}
+                        type="submit"
+                        className={`
+                            relative overflow-hidden group bg-zinc-900 text-white px-10 py-4 rounded-full font-medium text-lg shadow-xl shadow-zinc-200 transition-all duration-300
+                            disabled:opacity-70 disabled:cursor-not-allowed hover:bg-zinc-800 active:scale-95 w-full sm:w-auto
+                        `}
+                    >
+                        <span className="relative z-10 flex items-center gap-3">
+                            {loading || (response_data.uploaded && !response_data.got_result) ? (
+                                <>
+                                    <span>{response_data.uploaded ? "Analyzing" : "Uploading"}</span>
+                                    <LoadingCircle className="size-5 animate-spin text-white/50" />
+                                </>
+                            ) : (
+                                "Start Analysis"
+                            )}
+                        </span>
+                    </button>
+                </div>
+            </div>
+
+            {/* --- RIGHT COLUMN: Settings --- */}
+            <div className='w-full lg:w-96 flex flex-col gap-8'>
+
+                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
+                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-zinc-900 tracking-tight">Configuration</h2>
+                    <p className="text-zinc-500 text-sm">Select the detection models to run on your file.</p>
+                </div>
+
+                {/* Analysis Selection Grid */}
+                <div className={`transition-all duration-500 ${file ? 'opacity-100 translate-y-0' : 'opacity-40 translate-y-4 grayscale pointer-events-none'}`}>
+
+                    {/* VIDEO & AUDIO GROUP */}
+                    <div className={`grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 ${uploadType === 'image' ? 'hidden' : 'block'}`}>
+                        <div className={uploadType === 'audio' ? 'hidden' : 'block'}>
+                            <AnalysisCard
+                                active={analysisTypes["frameCheck"]}
+                                onClick={() => handleAnalysisTypeChange("frameCheck")}
+                                icon={PhotoVideo}
+                                title="Deepfake Video"
+                                desc="Frame-by-frame analysis to detect synthetic manipulations."
+                            />
+                        </div>
+
+                        <div className={uploadType === 'video' ? 'block' : 'block'}> {/* Always show audio option unless image */}
+                            <AnalysisCard
+                                active={analysisTypes["audioAnalysis"]}
+                                onClick={() => handleAnalysisTypeChange("audioAnalysis")}
+                                icon={Waveform}
+                                title="Audio Spoof"
+                                desc="Spectral analysis to identify AI-generated voice patterns."
+                            />
+                        </div>
+                    </div>
+
+                    {/* IMAGE GROUP */}
+                    <div className={uploadType === 'video' || uploadType === 'audio' ? 'hidden' : 'block'}>
+                        <AnalysisCard
+                            active={analysisTypes["aigcCheck"]}
+                            onClick={() => handleAnalysisTypeChange("aigcCheck")}
+                            icon={ImageSearch}
+                            title="AIGC Image"
+                            desc="Pixel-level inspection to reveal generative AI artifacts."
+                        />
+                    </div>
+
+                    {/* Info Helper */}
+                    <div className="mt-6 p-4 bg-zinc-50 rounded-xl border border-zinc-100 flex gap-3 text-sm text-zinc-500">
+                        <InfoCircle className="size-5 flex-shrink-0 text-zinc-400" />
+                        <p>Analysis time varies by file size. Results will appear automatically upon completion.</p>
+                    </div>
+
+                </div>
+            </div>
+        </form>
+    )
+}
+
+export default Form;

+import jsPDF from "jspdf";
+import html2canvas from 'html2canvas';
+import { Outfit_bold_font } from './outfit-bold-font';
+import { Outfit_normal_font } from './outfit-normal-font';
+import { logo_base64 } from './logo_base64';
+
+// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
+// CONSTANTS & CONFIG
+// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+const COLORS = {
+    // Brand / Neutral
+    text: [24, 24, 27],        // Zinc 900
+    secondary: [113, 113, 122],// Zinc 500
+    border: [228, 228, 231],   // Zinc 200
+    bgLight: [250, 250, 250],  // Zinc 50
+    white: [255, 255, 255],
+
+    // Semantic
+    emerald: [16, 185, 129],   // Emerald 500
+    emeraldLight: [209, 250, 229], // Emerald 100
+    rose: [244, 63, 94],       // Rose 500
+    roseLight: [255, 228, 230], // Rose 100
+
+    // UI
+    brandBlue: [2, 83, 228],
+};
+
+const GEOM = {
+    pageW: 8.27,      // A4 Width (in)
+    pageH: 11.69,     // A4 Height (in)
+    margin: 0.6,      // Page Margin
+    cardRadius: 0.15, // Border radius for boxes
+};
+
+const CONTENT_WIDTH = GEOM.pageW - (2 * GEOM.margin);
+
+// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
+// HELPER FUNCTIONS
+// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+// Draw a chic rounded card
+const drawCard = (doc, x, y, w, h, color = COLORS.white) => {
+    doc.setFillColor(...color);
+    doc.setDrawColor(...COLORS.border);
+    doc.setLineWidth(1 / 72); // Thin stroke
+    doc.roundedRect(x, y, w, h, GEOM.cardRadius, GEOM.cardRadius, 'FD');
+};
+
+// Draw a status badge (e.g., "Authentic")
+const drawBadge = (doc, text, x, y, type = "neutral") => {
+    const fontSize = 10;
+    doc.setFontSize(fontSize);
+    doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+
+    const textW = doc.getTextWidth(text);
+    const padX = 0.15;
+    const padY = 0.05;
+    const badgeW = textW + (padX * 2);
+    const badgeH = (fontSize / 72) + (padY * 2) + 0.02;
+
+    let bgColor = COLORS.bgLight;
+    let textColor = COLORS.secondary;
+
+    if (type === "success") {
+        bgColor = COLORS.emeraldLight;
+        textColor = [6, 95, 70]; // Emerald 800
+    } else if (type === "danger") {
+        bgColor = COLORS.roseLight;
+        textColor = [136, 19, 55]; // Rose 900
+    }
+
+    doc.setFillColor(...bgColor);
+    doc.setDrawColor(...bgColor); // No border or same color
+    doc.roundedRect(x, y - (fontSize / 72) - padY + 0.05, badgeW, badgeH, 0.05, 0.05, 'F');
+
+    doc.setTextColor(...textColor);
+    doc.text(text, x + padX, y + 0.05);
+
+    return badgeW; // Returns width for layout
+};
+
+// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
+// MAIN EXPORT FUNCTION
+// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+export const video_pdf = async (analysisTypes, results, result_values, file_metadata, audio_graph_Ref, frame_person_refs, fileUrl = null) => {
+
+    if (!results || !result_values) {
+        console.error("Analysis results are not ready for export.");
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: "p", unit: "in", compress: true });
+
+    // 1. Setup Fonts
+    doc.addFileToVFS('outfit_normal.ttf', Outfit_normal_font);
+    doc.addFileToVFS('outfit_bold.ttf', Outfit_bold_font);
+    doc.addFont('outfit_normal.ttf', 'Outfit', 'normal');
+    doc.addFont('outfit_bold.ttf', 'Outfit', 'bold');
+
+    let curr_y = GEOM.margin;
+    let curr_x = GEOM.margin;
+
+    // ============================================
+    // HEADER
+    // ============================================
+
+    // Logo
+    const logoSize = 0.5;
+    doc.addImage(logo_base64, 'PNG', curr_x, curr_y - 0.1, logoSize, logoSize);
+
+    // Company Name
+    curr_x += logoSize + 0.15;
+    doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+    doc.setFontSize(24);
+    doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.brandBlue);
+    doc.text('Contrails AI', curr_x, curr_y + 0.25);
+
+    // Report Title (Right Aligned)
+    doc.setFontSize(20);
+    doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+    const title = "Deepfake Analysis Report";
+    doc.text(title, GEOM.pageW - GEOM.margin - doc.getTextWidth(title), curr_y + 0.25);
+
+    curr_y += 0.8;
+    curr_x = GEOM.margin;
+
+    // Horizontal Line
+    doc.setDrawColor(...COLORS.border);
+    doc.setLineWidth(1 / 72);
+    doc.line(curr_x, curr_y, GEOM.pageW - GEOM.margin, curr_y);
+    curr_y += 0.4;
+
+    // ============================================
+    // FILE METADATA SECTION
+    // ============================================
+
+    doc.setFontSize(12);
+    doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+    doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+    doc.text("File Overview", curr_x, curr_y);
+    curr_y += 0.2;
+
+    const metaCardH = 1.6;
+    drawCard(doc, curr_x, curr_y, CONTENT_WIDTH, metaCardH, COLORS.bgLight);
+
+    // Inside Metadata Card
+    let metaY = curr_y + 0.4;
+    let metaX = curr_x + 0.3;
+
+    // Helper for key-value pair
+    const drawMetaItem = (label, value, x, y) => {
+        doc.setFontSize(9);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.secondary);
+        doc.text(label.toUpperCase(), x, y);
+
+        doc.setFontSize(11);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "normal");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+
+        // Handle long filenames with simple split
+        if (doc.getTextWidth(value) > 3.5) {
+            const splitText = doc.splitTextToSize(value, 3.5);
+            doc.text(splitText, x, y + 0.2);
+        } else {
+            doc.text(value, x, y + 0.2);
+        }
+    };
+
+    drawMetaItem("Filename", file_metadata.name, metaX, metaY);
+    drawMetaItem("File Size", file_metadata.size, metaX + 4.0, metaY); // Column 2
+
+    metaY += 0.7;
+    drawMetaItem("Date Analysis", new Date().toLocaleDateString(), metaX, metaY);
+    drawMetaItem("File Type", file_metadata.type || "Unknown", metaX + 4.0, metaY);
+
+    curr_y += metaCardH + 0.4;
+
+    // ============================================
+    // EXPERT COMMENT (If Exists)
+    // ============================================
+    if (file_metadata.verifier_comment) {
+        console.log("Printing Verifier Comment:", file_metadata.verifier_comment);
+
+        doc.setFontSize(12);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+        doc.text("Expert's Comment", curr_x, curr_y);
+        curr_y += 0.2;
+
+        doc.setFontSize(10);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "normal");
+
+        // Ensure text color is set to dark/black explicitly before printing on white card
+        doc.setTextColor(0, 0, 0);
+
+        const commentLines = doc.splitTextToSize(file_metadata.verifier_comment, CONTENT_WIDTH - 0.4);
+        // Calculate dynamic height based on lines (approx 0.2 unit per line) + padding
+        const commentH = (commentLines.length * 0.2) + 0.4;
+
+        drawCard(doc, curr_x, curr_y, CONTENT_WIDTH, commentH, COLORS.white);
+
+        // Adjust text position: x + padding, y + header padding + baseline offset
+        doc.text(commentLines, curr_x + 0.2, curr_y + 0.35);
+
+        curr_y += commentH + 0.4;
+    }
+
+    // ============================================
+    // HELPER: ANALYSIS HEADER (Consistent UI)
+    // ============================================
+    const drawAnalysisHeader = (title, isReal, score, imageBase64 = null) => {
+        const headerH = 0.8;
+        // const startY = curr_y;
+
+        let contentX = curr_x;
+
+        // 1. Thumbnail Image (if exists)
+        if (imageBase64) {
+            const imgSize = 0.6;
+            // Mask circle? jsPDF doesn't do circle clipping easily. We'll do a rounded rect or just a rect.
+            try {
+                doc.setDrawColor(...COLORS.border);
+                doc.roundedRect(contentX, curr_y, imgSize, imgSize, 0.15, 0.15, 'S');
+                doc.addImage(imageBase64, 'JPEG', contentX + 0.02, curr_y + 0.02, imgSize - 0.04, imgSize - 0.04);
+            } catch (e) {
+                console.warn("Could not draw face/thumbnail image", e);
+            }
+            contentX += imgSize + 0.25;
+        }
+
+        // 2. Title and Badge (Stacked or Inline)
+        doc.setFontSize(14);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+        doc.text(title, contentX, curr_y + 0.25);
+
+        drawBadge(doc, isReal ? "AUTHENTIC" : "SUSPICIOUS", contentX, curr_y + 0.5, isReal ? "success" : "danger");
+
+        // 3. Confidence Score (Right Aligned)
+        doc.setFontSize(10);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "normal");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.secondary);
+        const labelText = "CONFIDENCE";
+        doc.text(labelText, GEOM.pageW - GEOM.margin - doc.getTextWidth(labelText), curr_y + 0.25);
+
+        doc.setFontSize(22);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+        const scoreText = `${score}%`;
+        doc.text(scoreText, GEOM.pageW - GEOM.margin - doc.getTextWidth(scoreText), curr_y + 0.6);
+
+        curr_y += headerH + 0.2;
+    };
+
+    doc.setFontSize(12);
+    doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+    doc.text("Executive Summary", curr_x, curr_y);
+    curr_y += 0.2;
+
+    // Determine Overall Status for a big banner
+    // Logic: If ANY check fails (is suspicious), overall is suspicious.
+    let overallReal = true;
+    let overallScore = 0;
+
+    const analysisKeys = ["audioAnalysis", "frameCheck", "aigcCheck"];
+    let scores = [];
+
+    analysisKeys.forEach(key => {
+        if (result_values[key]) {
+            let isReal = false;
+            let score = 0;
+            if (key === "audioAnalysis") {
+                isReal = result_values[key].prediction;
+                score = Number(result_values[key].percentage);
+            } else if (key === "aigcCheck") {
+                isReal = result_values[key].prediction;
+                score = Number(result_values[key].percentage);
+            } else if (key === "frameCheck") {
+                const labels = Object.keys(result_values["frameCheck"]);
+                if (labels.length > 0) {
+                    const fakeLabel = labels.find(l => !result_values["frameCheck"][l].prediction);
+                    if (fakeLabel) {
+                        isReal = false;
+                        score = Number(result_values["frameCheck"][fakeLabel].percentage);
+                    } else {
+                        isReal = true;
+                        score = Number(result_values["frameCheck"][labels[0]].percentage);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (!isReal) overallReal = false;
+            scores.push(score);
+        }
+    });
+
+    const summaryHeight = 1.2;
+    // bg color based on verdict
+    const bannerColor = overallReal ? COLORS.emeraldLight : COLORS.roseLight;
+    const bannerBorder = overallReal ? [6, 95, 70] : [136, 19, 55]; // darker shade
+
+    doc.setFillColor(...bannerColor);
+    doc.setDrawColor(...bannerColor);
+    doc.roundedRect(curr_x, curr_y, CONTENT_WIDTH, summaryHeight, 0.2, 0.2, "F");
+
+    // Banner Text
+    doc.setFontSize(16);
+    doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+    doc.setTextColor(...bannerBorder);
+    const verdictText = overallReal ? "NO MANIPULATION DETECTED" : "POTENTIAL MANIPULATION DETECTED";
+    doc.text(verdictText, curr_x + 0.3, curr_y + 0.5);
+
+    doc.setFontSize(11);
+    doc.setFont("Outfit", "normal");
+    doc.text(overallReal ? "The analysis indicates the media is likely authentic." : "The analysis detected signs of synthetic manipulation.", curr_x + 0.3, curr_y + 0.8);
+
+    curr_y += summaryHeight + 0.5;
+
+    // ============================================
+    // DETAILED ANALYSIS SECTIONS
+    // ============================================
+
+    // HELPER: New Page Trigger
+    const checkPageBreak = (neededHeight) => {
+        if (curr_y + neededHeight > GEOM.pageH - GEOM.margin) {
+            doc.addPage();
+            curr_y = GEOM.margin;
+            return true;
+        }
+        return false;
+    };
+
+    // 1. IMAGE ANALYSIS (Prioritize if exists)
+    if (result_values["aigcCheck"] && fileUrl) {
+        doc.addPage();
+        curr_y = GEOM.margin;
+
+        doc.setFontSize(16);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+        doc.text("Image Analysis", curr_x, curr_y);
+        curr_y += 0.4;
+
+        const imgDataVal = result_values["aigcCheck"];
+        const isReal = imgDataVal.prediction;
+        const confScore = isReal ? imgDataVal.percentage : (100 - Number(imgDataVal.percentage)).toFixed(2);
+
+        // Header
+        drawAnalysisHeader("Analyzed Image", isReal, confScore, null);
+
+        // Draw Image Logic - Large Preview
+        if (fileUrl) {
+            try {
+                // Determine layout
+                const maxImgH = 5.0; // Max height for image
+                const maxImgW = CONTENT_WIDTH;
+
+                // We'll create an Image object to get dimensions, but since we are async...
+                // Ideally this should be passed in or we load it. 
+                // However, jspdf addImage can handle URL but synchronous is tricky if we don't know dims.
+                // We can just use a fixed aspect or try to load.
+                // For robustness, let's load it into an HTML Image element to get natural dims.
+                const img = new Image();
+                img.src = fileUrl;
+                await new Promise((resolve) => {
+                    if (img.complete) resolve();
+                    else {
+                        img.onload = resolve;
+                        img.onerror = resolve;
+                    }
+                });
+
+                if (img.width) {
+                    let finalW = maxImgW;
+                    let finalH = (img.height * finalW) / img.width;
+
+                    if (finalH > maxImgH) {
+                        finalH = maxImgH;
+                        finalW = (img.width * finalH) / img.height;
+                    }
+
+                    // Center it if width < content width
+                    const xOffset = (CONTENT_WIDTH - finalW) / 2;
+
+                    doc.addImage(fileUrl, 'JPEG', curr_x + xOffset, curr_y, finalW, finalH);
+                    curr_y += finalH + 0.4;
+                }
+            } catch (e) {
+                console.error("Could not load main image for PDF", e);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // 2. AUDIO ANALYSIS
+    if (result_values["audioAnalysis"] && audio_graph_Ref.current) {
+        doc.addPage();
+        curr_y = GEOM.margin;
+
+        // Header
+        doc.setFontSize(16);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text); // Section Title Color
+        doc.text("Audio Analysis", curr_x, curr_y);
+        curr_y += 0.4;
+
+        // Data
+        const audData = result_values["audioAnalysis"];
+        const isReal = audData.prediction;
+        const confScore = isReal ? audData.percentage : (100 - Number(audData.percentage)).toFixed(2);
+
+        // Use Consistent Header (No image for audio typically)
+        drawAnalysisHeader("Audio Stream", isReal, confScore, null);
+
+        // Capture Graph
+        try {
+            const canvas = await html2canvas(audio_graph_Ref.current);
+            const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
+            const imgW = CONTENT_WIDTH; // Full width
+            const imgH = (canvas.height * imgW) / canvas.width;
+
+            // Draw clean border around graph
+            doc.setDrawColor(...COLORS.border);
+            doc.rect(curr_x, curr_y, imgW, imgH, 'S');
+            doc.addImage(imgData, 'PNG', curr_x, curr_y, imgW, imgH, '', 'FAST');
+
+            curr_y += imgH + 0.4;
+        } catch (e) {
+            console.error("Audio graph capture failed", e);
+            doc.text("[Graph Caption Failed]", curr_x, curr_y);
+            curr_y += 0.5;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // 3. FRAME ANALYSIS
+    if (result_values["frameCheck"]) {
+        doc.addPage();
+        curr_y = GEOM.margin;
+
+        doc.setFontSize(16);
+        doc.setFont("Outfit", "bold");
+        doc.setTextColor(...COLORS.text);
+        doc.text("Visual Analysis", curr_x, curr_y);
+        curr_y += 0.4;
+
+        // Deepfake face detections
+        if (result_values["frameCheck"]["prediction"] === undefined) {
+            // Iterate through top 3 faces as per UI
+            const sortedLabels = Object.keys(result_values["frameCheck"])
+                // Logic must match UI exactly to align with refs
+                // UI: filter face_labels -> sort -> slice(0,3)
+                .filter((v) => !results["face_labels"] || results["face_labels"].length === 0 || results["face_labels"].includes(v))
+                .sort((a, b) => result_values["frameCheck"][b]["data_points"] - result_values["frameCheck"][a]["data_points"])
+                .slice(0, 3);
+
+            for (let i = 0; i < sortedLabels.length; i++) {
+                const label = sortedLabels[i];
+                const data = result_values["frameCheck"][label];
+                const ref = frame_person_refs[i];
+
+                if (!data || !ref.current) continue;
+
+                const isReal = data.prediction;
+                const confScore = isReal ? data.percentage : (100 - Number(data.percentage)).toFixed(2);
+
+                const faceImage = results?.frameCheck?.label_faces ? results.frameCheck.label_faces[label] : null;
+
+                checkPageBreak(5); // Header + Graph
+
+                // Use Consistent Header
+                drawAnalysisHeader(`Subject ${i + 1}`, isReal, confScore, faceImage);
+
+                try {
+                    const canvas = await html2canvas(ref.current);
+                    const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
+                    const imgW = CONTENT_WIDTH;
+                    const imgH = (canvas.height * imgW) / canvas.width;
+
+                    // doc.setDrawColor(...COLORS.border);
+                    // doc.rect(curr_x, curr_y, imgW, imgH, 'S');
+                    doc.addImage(imgData, 'PNG', curr_x, curr_y, imgW, imgH, '', 'FAST');
+
+                    curr_y += imgH + 0.4;
+                } catch (e) {
+                    console.error("Frame graph capture failed", e);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // AIGC detections
+        else {
+            const data = result_values["frameCheck"];
+            const ref = frame_person_refs[0];
+            const isReal = data.prediction;
+            const confScore = isReal ? data.percentage : (100 - Number(data.percentage)).toFixed(2);
+
+            checkPageBreak(5); // Header + Graph
+
+            // Use Consistent Header
+            drawAnalysisHeader("AIGC Video Detection", isReal, confScore, null);
+
+            try {
+                const canvas = await html2canvas(ref.current);
+                const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
+                const imgW = CONTENT_WIDTH;
+                const imgH = (canvas.height * imgW) / canvas.width;
+
+                // doc.setDrawColor(...COLORS.border);
+                // doc.rect(curr_x, curr_y, imgW, imgH, 'S');
+                doc.addImage(imgData, 'PNG', curr_x, curr_y, imgW, imgH, '', 'FAST');
+
+                curr_y += imgH + 0.4;
+            } catch (e) {
+                console.error("Frame graph capture failed", e);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Save
+    doc.save(`${file_metadata.name}_Report.pdf`);
+};
